 user_id uuid PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
);
ALTER TABLE parapost_groups_private.test_users ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
REVOKE ALL ON parapost_groups_private.test_users FROM PUBLIC,anon,authenticated;

ALTER TABLE public.group_posts ADD COLUMN published boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false;
CREATE TABLE parapost_groups_private.media_cleanup (
  storage_path text PRIMARY KEY, queued_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
ALTER TABLE parapost_groups_private.media_cleanup ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
REVOKE ALL ON parapost_groups_private.media_cleanup FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated;

CREATE FUNCTION parapost_groups_private.enabled() RETURNS boolean
LANGUAGE sql STABLE SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = '' AS $$
  SELECT auth.uid() IS NOT NULL AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM auth.users WHERE id = auth.uid())
    AND coalesce(auth.jwt()->>'is_anonymous','false')<>'true'
    AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM parapost_groups_private.feature_gate WHERE enabled
      AND (allow_all_authenticated OR EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM parapost_groups_private.test_users WHERE user_id=auth.uid())));
$$;
CREATE FUNCTION parapost_groups_private.blocked(a uuid, b uuid) RETURNS boolean
LANGUAGE sql STABLE SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = '' AS $$
  SELECT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.blocked_users WHERE (blocker_id=a AND blocked_id=b) OR (blocker_id=b AND blocked_id=a))
      OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.user_blocks WHERE (blocker_id=a AND blocked_id=b) OR (blocker_id=b AND blocked_id=a));
$$;
-- Levels: directory, read, member, admin, moderate, owner. Denials override public visibility.
CREATE FUNCTION parapost_groups_private.allowed(gid uuid, level text) RETURNS boolean
LANGUAGE sql STABLE SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = '' AS $$
 SELECT parapost_groups_private.enabled() AND EXISTS (
   SELECT 1 FROM public.groups g LEFT JOIN public.group_members m
     ON m.group_id=g.id AND m.user_id=auth.uid()
   WHERE g.id=gid AND NOT parapost_groups_private.blocked(auth.uid(),g.owner_id)
   AND CASE level
     WHEN 'directory' THEN true
     WHEN 'read' THEN coalesce(m.status NOT IN ('rejected','removed','banned'),true)
       AND (g.visibility='public' OR m.status='approved')
     WHEN 'member' THEN m.status='approved'
     WHEN 'admin' THEN m.status='approved' AND m.role IN ('owner','admin')
     WHEN 'moderate' THEN m.status='approved' AND m.role IN ('owner','admin','moderator')
     WHEN 'owner' THEN m.status='approved' AND m.role='owner' AND g.owner_id=auth.uid()
     ELSE false END
 );
$$;

-- Queue physical object removal transactionally. Never DELETE storage.objects via SQL.
CREATE FUNCTION parapost_groups_private.queue_media_cleanup() RETURNS trigger
LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = '' AS $$
BEGIN
 IF TG_TABLE_NAME='group_post_media' THEN
   INSERT INTO parapost_groups_private.media_cleanup(storage_path) VALUES (OLD.storage_path) ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING;
 ELSE
   IF OLD.cover_storage_path IS NOT NULL AND (TG_OP='DELETE' OR OLD.cover_storage_path IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.cover_storage_path) THEN
     INSERT INTO parapost_groups_private.media_cleanup(storage_path) VALUES (OLD.cover_storage_path) ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING;
   END IF;
 END IF;
 RETURN OLD;
END; $$;
CREATE TRIGGER groups_queue_cover AFTER UPDATE OR DELETE ON public.groups FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION parapost_groups_private.queue_media_cleanup();
CREATE TRIGGER groups_queue_media AFTER DELETE ON public.group_post_media FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION parapost_groups_private.queue_media_cleanup();

CREATE FUNCTION parapost_groups_private.guard_owner_delete() RETURNS trigger
LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = '' AS $$
BEGIN
 -- Parent is already absent during a legitimate ON DELETE CASCADE.
 IF OLD.role='owner' AND EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM public.groups WHERE id=OLD.group_id) THEN
   RAISE EXCEPTION 'Delete the Group instead of its owner membership';
 END IF;
 RETURN OLD;
END; $$;
CREATE TRIGGER group_members_protect_owner_delete BEFORE DELETE ON public.group_members FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION parapost_groups_private.guard_owner_delete();

-- Retire old entry points; keep their implementations as preserved draft history.
REVOKE EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.request_group_membership(uuid) FROM authenticated;
REVOKE EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.review_group_membership(uuid,uuid,text) FROM authenticated;

CREATE FUNCTION parapost_groups_private.command(action text, gid uuid, payload jsonb)
RETURNS jsonb LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = '' AS $$
DECLARE
 uid uuid := auth.uid(); g public.groups; m public.group_members; p public.group_posts;
 target uuid; pid uuid; mid uuid; path text; mime text; role_name text; result jsonb; item record;
BEGIN
 IF NOT parapost_groups_private.enabled() THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Groups unavailable' USING ERRCODE='42501'; END IF;
 IF action='create' THEN
   IF (SELECT count(*) FROM public.groups WHERE owner_id=uid)>=100 THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Group creation limit reached'; END IF;
   INSERT INTO public.groups(owner_id,name,description,visibility)
   VALUES(uid,btrim(payload->>'name'),coalesce(payload->>'description',''),coalesce(payload->>'visibility','public')) RETURNING * INTO g;
   RETURN jsonb_build_object('id',g.id);
 END IF;
 -- Group lock serializes ALL writes, including membership removal vs publish/role changes.
 SELECT * INTO g FROM public.groups WHERE id=gid FOR UPDATE;
 IF NOT FOUND OR NOT parapost_groups_private.allowed(gid,'directory') THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Group unavailable' USING ERRCODE='42501'; END IF;
 SELECT * INTO m FROM public.group_members WHERE group_id=gid AND user_id=uid;
 IF action='join' THEN
   IF m.status IS NOT NULL THEN
     IF m.status NOT IN ('pending','approved') THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Membership unavailable'; END IF;
   ELSE
     INSERT INTO public.group_members(group_id,user_id,role,status,approved_at)
     VALUES(gid,uid,'member',CASE WHEN g.visibility='public' THEN 'approved' ELSE 'pending' END,CASE WHEN g.visibility='public' THEN now() END);
   END IF;
 ELSIF action='leave' THEN
   IF m.role='owner' THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Owner must delete the Group; transfer is not supported'; END IF;
   -- Only voluntary departures remove the row. Removed/banned/rejected rows persist.
   DELETE FROM public.group_members WHERE group_id=gid AND user_id=uid AND status IN ('pending','approved');
 ELSIF action IN ('approve','reject','remove','ban','role','restore') THEN
   target := (payload->>'user_id')::uuid;
   IF NOT parapost_groups_private.allowed(gid,'admin') OR target=uid OR target=g.owner_id THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Not authorized' USING ERRCODE='42501'; END IF;
   SELECT * INTO m FROM public.group_members WHERE group_id=gid AND user_id=target;
   IF NOT FOUND THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Membership unavailable'; END IF;
   IF NOT parapost_groups_private.allowed(gid,'owner') AND (m.role <> 'member' OR action IN ('role','restore')) THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Owner permission required'; END IF;
   IF action IN ('approve','restore') AND parapost_groups_private.blocked(uid,target) THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Membership unavailable'; END IF;
   IF action IN ('approve','restore') AND parapost_groups_private.blocked(g.owner_id,target) THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Membership unavailable'; END IF;
   IF action IN ('approve','reject') THEN
     IF m.status <> 'pending' THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Request is no longer pending'; END IF;
     UPDATE public.group_members SET status=CASE WHEN action='approve' THEN 'approved' ELSE 'rejected' END,
       approved_at=CASE WHEN action='approve' THEN now() END WHERE group_id=gid AND user_id=target;
   ELSIF action='role' THEN
     role_name := payload->>'role';
     IF m.status <> 'approved' OR role_name IS NULL OR role_name NOT IN ('admin','moderator','member') THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Invalid role change'; END IF;
     UPDATE public.group_members SET role=role_name WHERE group_id=gid AND user_id=target;
   ELSIF action='restore' THEN
     IF m.status NOT IN ('removed','rejected','banned') THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Invalid restoration'; END IF;
     -- Restoration requires owner action AND a fresh approval, never immediate access.
     UPDATE public.group_members SET role='member',status='pending',approved_at=NULL WHERE group_id=gid AND user_id=target;
   ELSE
     UPDATE public.group_members SET role='member',status=CASE WHEN action='ban' THEN 'banned' ELSE 'removed' END,approved_at=NULL WHERE group_id=gid AND user_id=target;
   END IF;
 ELSIF action='edit' THEN
   IF NOT parapost_groups_private.allowed(gid,'admin') THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Not authorized'; END IF;
   -- Privacy changes are owner-only. Existing approvals survive; pending requests stay pending.
   IF payload->>'visibility' IS DISTINCT FROM g.visibility AND NOT parapost_groups_private.allowed(gid,'owner') THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Owner permission required'; END IF;
   UPDATE public.groups SET name=btrim(payload->>'name'),description=coalesce(payload->>'description',''),visibility=payload->>'visibility',updated_at=now() WHERE id=gid;
 ELSIF action='delete' THEN
   IF NOT parapost_groups_private.allowed(gid,'owner') OR payload->>'confirmation' IS DISTINCT FROM g.name THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Owner and Group name confirmation required'; END IF;
   DELETE FROM public.groups WHERE id=gid;
 ELSIF action='draft' THEN
   IF NOT parapost_groups_private.allowed(gid,'member') THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Approved membership required'; END IF;
   IF (SELECT count(*) FROM public.group_posts WHERE group_id=gid AND author_id=uid AND NOT published)>=10 THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Too many unfinished uploads'; END IF;
   INSERT INTO public.group_posts(group_id,author_id,content) VALUES(gid,uid,coalesce(payload->>'content','')) RETURNING id INTO pid;
   RETURN jsonb_build_object('id',pid);
 ELSIF action IN ('reserve_media','publish','edit_post','delete_post') THEN
   pid := (payload->>'post_id')::uuid;
   SELECT * INTO p FROM public.group_posts WHERE id=pid AND group_id=gid;
   IF NOT FOUND OR p.is_deleted THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Post unavailable'; END IF;
   IF NOT parapost_groups_private.allowed(gid,'member') OR (p.author_id<>uid AND NOT (action='delete_post' AND parapost_groups_private.allowed(gid,'moderate'))) THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Not authorized'; END IF;
   IF action='delete_post' THEN
     DELETE FROM public.group_posts WHERE id=pid;
   ELSIF action='edit_post' THEN
     IF char_length(btrim(coalesce(payload->>'content','')))=0 AND NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM public.group_post_media WHERE post_id=pid) THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Post cannot be empty'; END IF;
     UPDATE public.group_posts SET content=coalesce(payload->>'content',''),updated_at=now() WHERE id=pid;
   ELSIF action='reserve_media' THEN
     IF p.published OR (SELECT count(*) FROM public.group_post_media WHERE post_id=pid)>=4 THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Media limit or published post'; END IF;
     mime := payload->>'mime_type';
     IF mime IS NULL OR mime NOT IN ('image/jpeg','image/png','image/webp','video/mp4','video/webm') THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Unsupported media'; END IF;
     mid := gen_random_uuid();
     path := gid::text || '/posts/' || pid::text || '/' || uid::text || '/' || mid::text;
     INSERT INTO public.group_post_media(id,group_id,post_id,uploader_id,media_type,storage_path,mime_type,display_order)
     VALUES(mid,gid,pid,uid,CASE WHEN mime LIKE 'image/%' THEN 'image' ELSE 'video' END,path,mime,(SELECT count(*) FROM public.group_post_media WHERE post_id=pid));
     RETURN jsonb_build_object('path',path);
   ELSE
     IF p.published THEN RETURN jsonb_build_object('id',pid); END IF;
     IF char_length(btrim(p.content))=0 AND NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM public.group_post_media WHERE post_id=pid) THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Post cannot be empty'; END IF;
     FOR item IN SELECT * FROM public.group_post_media WHERE post_id=pid LOOP
       IF NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM storage.objects o WHERE o.bucket_id='group-media' AND o.name=item.storage_path
         AND o.metadata->>'mimetype'=item.mime_type AND (o.metadata->>'size')::bigint BETWEEN 1 AND CASE WHEN item.media_type='image' THEN 8388608 ELSE 33554432 END) THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Media upload incomplete or invalid'; END IF;
     END LOOP;
     UPDATE public.group_posts SET published=true,updated_at=now() WHERE id=pid;
   END IF;
 ELSIF action='remove_cover' THEN
   IF NOT parapost_groups_private.allowed(gid,'admin') THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Not authorized'; END IF;
   UPDATE public.groups SET cover_storage_path=NULL,updated_at=now() WHERE id=gid;
 ELSIF action='reserve_cover' THEN
   IF NOT parapost_groups_private.allowed(gid,'admin') THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Not authorized'; END IF;
   IF (SELECT count(*) FROM parapost_groups_private.cover_uploads WHERE group_id=gid AND uploader_id=uid)>=10 THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Too many unfinished covers'; END IF;
   -- Covers are intentionally directory metadata, including those for private Groups.
   path := gid::text || '/covers/' || gen_random_uuid()::text;
   INSERT INTO parapost_groups_private.cover_uploads(storage_path,group_id,uploader_id) VALUES(path,gid,uid);
   RETURN jsonb_build_object('path',path);
 ELSIF action='set_cover' THEN
   path := payload->>'path';
   IF NOT parapost_groups_private.allowed(gid,'admin') OR NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM parapost_groups_private.cover_uploads c JOIN storage.objects o ON o.name=c.storage_path AND o.bucket_id='group-media'
     WHERE c.storage_path=path AND c.group_id=gid AND c.uploader_id=uid AND o.metadata->>'mimetype' IN ('image/jpeg','image/png','image/webp') AND (o.metadata->>'size')::bigint BETWEEN 1 AND 8388608) THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Invalid cover'; END IF;
   UPDATE public.groups SET cover_storage_path=path,updated_at=now() WHERE id=gid;
   DELETE FROM parapost_groups_private.cover_uploads WHERE storage_path=path;
 ELSE RAISE EXCEPTION 'Unknown Group action';
 END IF;
 RETURN jsonb_build_object('id',gid);
END; $$;

CREATE TABLE parapost_groups_private.cover_uploads (
 storage_path text PRIMARY KEY, group_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES public.groups(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
 uploader_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE, created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
ALTER TABLE parapost_groups_private.cover_uploads ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
REVOKE ALL ON parapost_groups_private.cover_uploads FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated;
-- Cover reservations are queued too, but never clean an actively attached cover.
CREATE FUNCTION parapost_groups_private.queue_cover_reservation() RETURNS trigger LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path='' AS $$
BEGIN
 IF NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM public.groups WHERE cover_storage_path=OLD.storage_path) THEN
   INSERT INTO parapost_groups_private.media_cleanup(storage_path) VALUES(OLD.storage_path) ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING;
 END IF;
 RETURN OLD;
END; $$;
CREATE TRIGGER groups_queue_cover_reservation AFTER DELETE ON parapost_groups_private.cover_uploads FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION parapost_groups_private.queue_cover_reservation();

CREATE FUNCTION public.groups_available() RETURNS boolean
LANGUAGE sql STABLE SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path='' AS $$
 SELECT parapost_groups_private.enabled();
$$;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.groups_available() FROM PUBLIC,anon,authenticated;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.groups_available() TO authenticated;

CREATE FUNCTION public.groups_command(p_action text,p_group_id uuid DEFAULT NULL,p_payload jsonb DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb)
RETURNS jsonb LANGUAGE sql SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path='' AS $$
 SELECT parapost_groups_private.command(p_action,p_group_id,p_payload);
$$;

CREATE FUNCTION public.groups_directory(p_search text DEFAULT '',p_filter text DEFAULT 'all',p_group_id uuid DEFAULT NULL,p_offset integer DEFAULT 0)
RETURNS jsonb LANGUAGE sql STABLE SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path='' AS $$
 SELECT coalesce(jsonb_agg(row_to_json(x)),'[]'::jsonb) FROM (
   SELECT g.*,coalesce(pr.display_name,pr.username,'Member') AS owner_name,
     m.status AS membership_status,m.role AS membership_role,
     (SELECT count(*) FROM public.group_members c WHERE c.group_id=g.id AND c.status='approved' AND NOT parapost_groups_private.blocked(c.user_id,g.owner_id)) AS member_count
   FROM public.groups g LEFT JOIN public.group_members m ON m.group_id=g.id AND m.user_id=auth.uid()
   LEFT JOIN public.profiles pr ON pr.id=g.owner_id
   WHERE parapost_groups_private.allowed(g.id,'directory') AND (p_group_id IS NULL OR g.id=p_group_id)
     AND (p_filter='all' OR p_filter=g.visibility OR (p_filter='mine' AND m.status='approved'))
     AND (g.name || ' ' || g.description) ILIKE '%' || left(coalesce(p_search,''),100) || '%'
   ORDER BY g.created_at DESC,g.id LIMIT 24 OFFSET greatest(0,least(coalesce(p_offset,0),100000))
 ) x;
$$;
CREATE FUNCTION public.groups_members(p_group_id uuid,p_offset integer DEFAULT 0)
RETURNS jsonb LANGUAGE sql STABLE SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path='' AS $$
 SELECT coalesce(jsonb_agg(row_to_json(x)),'[]'::jsonb) FROM (
   SELECT m.*,coalesce(p.display_name,p.username,'Member') AS display_name
   FROM public.group_members m LEFT JOIN public.profiles p ON p.id=m.user_id
   WHERE m.group_id=p_group_id AND parapost_groups_private.allowed(p_group_id,'admin')
   ORDER BY (m.status='pending') DESC,m.created_at,m.user_id LIMIT 50 OFFSET greatest(0,least(coalesce(p_offset,0),100000))
 ) x;
$$;

-- SELECT-only data API. No direct table writes, even when a caller guesses IDs.
GRANT SELECT ON public.groups,public.group_members,public.group_posts,public.group_post_media TO authenticated;
CREATE POLICY groups_read ON public.groups FOR SELECT TO authenticated USING(parapost_groups_private.allowed(id,'directory'));
CREATE POLICY group_members_read ON public.group_members FOR SELECT TO authenticated USING(parapost_groups_private.allowed(group_id,'directory') AND (user_id=auth.uid() OR parapost_groups_private.allowed(group_id,'admin')));
CREATE POLICY group_posts_read ON public.group_posts FOR SELECT TO authenticated USING(NOT is_deleted AND parapost_groups_private.allowed(group_id,'read') AND (published OR (author_id=auth.uid() AND parapost_groups_private.allowed(group_id,'member'))));
CREATE POLICY group_post_media_read ON public.group_post_media FOR SELECT TO authenticated USING(EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM public.group_posts p WHERE p.id=post_id AND p.group_id=group_post_media.group_id));

CREATE FUNCTION parapost_groups_private.storage_allowed(path text, operation text) RETURNS boolean
LANGUAGE sql STABLE SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path='' AS $$
 SELECT parapost_groups_private.enabled() AND (
   CASE WHEN operation='read' THEN
     EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM public.groups g WHERE g.cover_storage_path=path AND parapost_groups_private.allowed(g.id,'directory'))
     OR EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM public.group_post_media m JOIN public.group_posts p ON p.id=m.post_id AND p.group_id=m.group_id
       WHERE m.storage_path=path AND NOT p.is_deleted AND parapost_groups_private.allowed(p.group_id,'read')
       AND (p.published OR (p.author_id=auth.uid() AND parapost_groups_private.allowed(p.group_id,'member'))))
   WHEN operation='insert' THEN
     EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM parapost_groups_private.cover_uploads c WHERE c.storage_path=path AND c.uploader_id=auth.uid() AND c.created_at>now()-interval '1 hour' AND parapost_groups_private.allowed(c.group_id,'admin'))
     OR EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM public.group_post_media m JOIN public.group_posts p ON p.id=m.post_id AND p.group_id=m.group_id
       WHERE m.storage_path=path AND m.uploader_id=auth.uid() AND p.author_id=auth.uid() AND NOT p.published AND NOT p.is_deleted AND p.created_at>now()-interval '1 hour' AND parapost_groups_private.allowed(p.group_id,'member'))
   ELSE false END
 );
$$;
UPDATE storage.buckets SET file_size_limit=33554432,allowed_mime_types=ARRAY['image/jpeg','image/png','image/webp','video/mp4','video/webm'] WHERE id='group-media' AND NOT public;
-- Prevent client changes to bucket privacy/configuration even under legacy policies.
CREATE POLICY groups_bucket_insert_guard ON storage.buckets AS RESTRICTIVE FOR INSERT TO PUBLIC WITH CHECK(id<>'group-media');
CREATE POLICY groups_bucket_update_guard ON storage.buckets AS RESTRICTIVE FOR UPDATE TO PUBLIC USING(id<>'group-media') WITH CHECK(id<>'group-media');
CREATE POLICY groups_bucket_delete_guard ON storage.buckets AS RESTRICTIVE FOR DELETE TO PUBLIC USING(id<>'group-media');
-- Restrictive policies AND against every legacy permissive policy. Other buckets unchanged.
CREATE POLICY groups_storage_read_guard ON storage.objects AS RESTRICTIVE FOR SELECT TO PUBLIC USING(bucket_id<>'group-media' OR (storage.allow_only_operation('object.get_authenticated') AND parapost_groups_private.storage_allowed(name,'read')));
CREATE POLICY groups_storage_insert_guard ON storage.objects AS RESTRICTIVE FOR INSERT TO PUBLIC WITH CHECK(bucket_id<>'group-media' OR (storage.allow_only_operation('object.upload') AND parapost_groups_private.storage_allowed(name,'insert')));
CREATE POLICY groups_storage_update_guard ON storage.objects AS RESTRICTIVE FOR UPDATE TO PUBLIC USING(bucket_id<>'group-media') WITH CHECK(bucket_id<>'group-media');
CREATE POLICY groups_storage_delete_guard ON storage.objects AS RESTRICTIVE FOR DELETE TO PUBLIC USING(bucket_id<>'group-media');
CREATE POLICY groups_storage_read ON storage.objects FOR SELECT TO authenticated USING(bucket_id='group-media' AND parapost_groups_private.storage_allowed(name,'read'));
CREATE POLICY groups_storage_insert ON storage.objects FOR INSERT TO authenticated WITH CHECK(bucket_id='group-media' AND parapost_groups_private.storage_allowed(name,'insert'));

-- Harden all private functions, including draft functions and trigger entry points.
REVOKE ALL ON ALL FUNCTIONS IN SCHEMA parapost_groups_private FROM PUBLIC,anon,authenticated;
GRANT USAGE ON SCHEMA parapost_groups_private TO anon,authenticated;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION parapost_groups_private.allowed(uuid,text),parapost_groups_private.storage_allowed(text,text) TO anon,authenticated;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.groups_command(text,uuid,jsonb),public.groups_directory(text,text,uuid,integer),public.groups_members(uuid,integer) FROM PUBLIC,anon,authenticated;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.groups_command(text,uuid,jsonb),public.groups_directory(text,text,uuid,integer),public.groups_members(uuid,integer) TO authenticated;

-- Operational cleanup API: executable only by service_role, never a browser role.
-- The accompanying script refuses production. Do not run without explicit approval.
CREATE FUNCTION public.groups_cleanup_batch() RETURNS SETOF text
LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path='' AS $$
DECLARE target record;
BEGIN
 FOR target IN SELECT g.id FROM public.groups g
 WHERE EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM public.group_posts p WHERE p.group_id=g.id AND NOT p.published AND p.created_at<now()-interval '24 hours')
    OR EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM parapost_groups_private.cover_uploads c WHERE c.group_id=g.id AND c.created_at<now()-interval '24 hours')
 ORDER BY g.id LIMIT 100 FOR UPDATE LOOP
   DELETE FROM public.group_posts WHERE group_id=target.id AND NOT published AND created_at<now()-interval '24 hours';
   DELETE FROM parapost_groups_private.cover_uploads WHERE group_id=target.id AND created_at<now()-interval '24 hours';
 END LOOP;
 RETURN QUERY SELECT q.storage_path FROM parapost_groups_private.media_cleanup q
 WHERE q.queued_at<now()-interval '24 hours'
   AND NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM public.groups g WHERE g.cover_storage_path=q.storage_path)
   AND NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM public.group_post_media m WHERE m.storage_path=q.storage_path)
   AND NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM parapost_groups_private.cover_uploads c WHERE c.storage_path=q.storage_path)
 ORDER BY q.queued_at LIMIT 100;
END; $$;
CREATE FUNCTION public.groups_cleanup_ack(p_paths text[]) RETURNS void
LANGUAGE sql SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path='' AS $$
 DELETE FROM parapost_groups_private.media_cleanup WHERE storage_path=ANY(p_paths);
$$;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.groups_cleanup_batch(),public.groups_cleanup_ack(text[]) FROM PUBLIC,anon,authenticated;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.groups_cleanup_batch(),public.groups_cleanup_ack(text[]) TO service_role;

COMMIT;

-- Group post interactions. No direct client writes.
CREATE TABLE public.group_post_likes (
  post_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES public.group_posts(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  user_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  PRIMARY KEY (post_id,user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE public.group_post_comments (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  post_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES public.group_posts(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  author_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  parent_id uuid,
  content text NOT NULL CHECK (char_length(btrim(content)) BETWEEN 1 AND 1200),
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  UNIQUE (id,post_id),
  FOREIGN KEY (parent_id,post_id)
    REFERENCES public.group_post_comments(id,post_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  CHECK (parent_id IS DISTINCT FROM id)
);

CREATE INDEX group_post_comments_feed_idx
  ON public.group_post_comments(post_id,created_at,id);

CREATE INDEX group_post_comments_author_idx
  ON public.group_post_comments(author_id);

ALTER TABLE public.group_post_likes ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE public.group_post_comments ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

REVOKE ALL ON public.group_post_likes,public.group_post_comments
  FROM PUBLIC,anon,authenticated;

GRANT SELECT ON public.group_post_likes,public.group_post_comments
  TO authenticated;

CREATE POLICY group_post_likes_read ON public.group_post_likes
FOR SELECT TO authenticated USING (
  EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM public.group_posts p
    WHERE p.id=post_id AND p.published AND NOT p.is_deleted
      AND parapost_groups_private.allowed(p.group_id,'read')
  )
);

CREATE POLICY group_post_comments_read ON public.group_post_comments
FOR SELECT TO authenticated USING (
  EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM public.group_posts p
    WHERE p.id=post_id AND p.published AND NOT p.is_deleted
      AND parapost_groups_private.allowed(p.group_id,'read')
  )
);

-- Likes for Group comments and replies.
-- The post_id is bound to the actual comment by a composite FK.
CREATE TABLE public.group_comment_likes (
  comment_id uuid NOT NULL,
  post_id uuid NOT NULL,
  user_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  PRIMARY KEY (comment_id,user_id),
  FOREIGN KEY (comment_id,post_id)
    REFERENCES public.group_post_comments(id,post_id)
    ON DELETE CASCADE
);

CREATE INDEX group_comment_likes_post_idx
  ON public.group_comment_likes(post_id,comment_id);

ALTER TABLE public.group_comment_likes ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

REVOKE ALL ON public.group_comment_likes
  FROM PUBLIC,anon,authenticated;

-- All interaction writes go through this authorized function.
CREATE FUNCTION public.groups_interact(
  p_group_id uuid, p_post_id uuid, p_action text,
  p_content text DEFAULT NULL, p_parent_id uuid DEFAULT NULL
) RETURNS jsonb
LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path='' AS $$
DECLARE
  v_uid uuid := auth.uid();
  v_group public.groups;
  v_post public.group_posts;
  v_parent public.group_post_comments;
  v_id uuid;
BEGIN
  IF v_uid IS NULL THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'Sign in required' USING ERRCODE='42501';
  END IF;

  -- Serialize interactions with Group membership/privacy changes.
  SELECT * INTO v_group FROM public.groups
    WHERE id=p_group_id FOR SHARE;

  IF NOT FOUND OR NOT parapost_groups_private.allowed(p_group_id,'member') THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'Approved membership required' USING ERRCODE='42501';
  END IF;

  SELECT * INTO v_post FROM public.group_posts
    WHERE id=p_post_id AND group_id=p_group_id
      AND published AND NOT is_deleted;

  IF NOT FOUND THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'Post unavailable' USING ERRCODE='42501';
  END IF;

  -- Block interactions with posts from blocked accounts.
  IF p_action IN ('like','comment','reply','like_comment')
     AND parapost_groups_private.blocked(v_uid,v_post.author_id) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'Interaction unavailable'
      USING ERRCODE='42501';
  END IF;

  IF p_action='like' THEN
    INSERT INTO public.group_post_likes(post_id,user_id)
      VALUES(p_post_id,v_uid)
      ON CONFLICT (post_id,user_id) DO NOTHING;
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('liked',true);
  ELSIF p_action='unlike' THEN
    DELETE FROM public.group_post_likes
      WHERE post_id=p_post_id AND user_id=v_uid;
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('liked',false);
  ELSIF p_action IN ('like_comment','unlike_comment') THEN
    IF p_parent_id IS NULL THEN
      RAISE EXCEPTION 'Comment required';
    END IF;

    SELECT * INTO v_parent
      FROM public.group_post_comments
      WHERE id=p_parent_id AND post_id=p_post_id;

    IF NOT FOUND THEN
      RAISE EXCEPTION 'Comment unavailable'
        USING ERRCODE='42501';
    END IF;

    IF p_action='like_comment' THEN
      IF parapost_groups_private.blocked(
        v_uid,v_parent.author_id
      ) THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Comment Like unavailable'
          USING ERRCODE='42501';
      END IF;

      INSERT INTO public.group_comment_likes
        (comment_id,post_id,user_id)
      VALUES (p_parent_id,p_post_id,v_uid)
      ON CONFLICT (comment_id,user_id) DO NOTHING;

      RETURN jsonb_build_object('liked',true);
    ELSE
      DELETE FROM public.group_comment_likes
      WHERE comment_id=p_parent_id AND user_id=v_uid;

      RETURN jsonb_build_object('liked',false);
    END IF;
  ELSIF p_action IN ('comment','reply') THEN
    IF p_content IS NULL OR
       char_length(btrim(p_content)) NOT BETWEEN 1 AND 1200 THEN
      RAISE EXCEPTION 'Invalid comment';
    END IF;

    IF p_action='comment' AND p_parent_id IS NOT NULL THEN
      RAISE EXCEPTION 'Invalid comment parent';
    END IF;

    IF p_action='reply' THEN
      IF p_parent_id IS NULL THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Reply parent required';
      END IF;
      SELECT * INTO v_parent FROM public.group_post_comments
        WHERE id=p_parent_id AND post_id=p_post_id;
      IF NOT FOUND OR v_parent.parent_id IS NOT NULL THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Invalid reply parent';
      END IF;
      -- Do not allow replies to blocked comment authors.
      IF parapost_groups_private.blocked(v_uid,v_parent.author_id) THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Reply unavailable'
          USING ERRCODE='42501';
      END IF;
    END IF;

    INSERT INTO public.group_post_comments
      (post_id,author_id,parent_id,content)
    VALUES (p_post_id,v_uid,p_parent_id,btrim(p_content))
    RETURNING id INTO v_id;

    RETURN jsonb_build_object('id',v_id);
  ELSE
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'Unknown interaction action';
  END IF;
END;
$$;

REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.groups_interact(uuid,uuid,text,text,uuid)
  FROM PUBLIC,anon,authenticated;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.groups_interact(uuid,uuid,text,text,uuid)
  TO authenticated;

-- Return authorized comment and reply Like statistics.
-- No list of members who liked comments is exposed.
CREATE FUNCTION public.groups_comment_like_stats(
  p_group_id uuid,
  p_post_id uuid
)
RETURNS TABLE (
  comment_id uuid,
  like_count bigint,
  liked boolean
)
LANGUAGE sql
STABLE
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = ''
AS $$
  WITH visible_comments AS (
    SELECT c.id, c.created_at
    FROM public.group_post_comments c
    JOIN public.group_posts p ON p.id = c.post_id
    WHERE c.post_id = p_post_id
      AND p.group_id = p_group_id
      AND p.published
      AND NOT p.is_deleted
      AND auth.uid() IS NOT NULL
      AND parapost_groups_private.allowed(
        p_group_id, 'read'
      )
    ORDER BY c.created_at, c.id
    LIMIT 100
  )
  SELECT
    c.id,
    (
      SELECT count(*)
      FROM public.group_comment_likes l
      WHERE l.comment_id = c.id
    ),
    EXISTS (
      SELECT 1
      FROM public.group_comment_likes l
      WHERE l.comment_id = c.id
        AND l.user_id = auth.uid()
    )
  FROM visible_comments c
  ORDER BY c.created_at, c.id;
$$;

REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION
  public.groups_comment_like_stats(uuid,uuid)
  FROM PUBLIC,anon,authenticated;

GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION
  public.groups_comment_like_stats(uuid,uuid)
  TO authenticated;


-- ====================================================
-- EXISTING MIGRATION: supabase/migrations/20261023050000_group_post_shares.sql
-- ====================================================

-- Parapost Network: Group post sharing.
-- LOCAL DRAFT ONLY. Staging review required before execution.
-- No direct client access. Read/write functions added separately.

BEGIN;

CREATE TABLE public.group_post_shares (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),

  group_id uuid NOT NULL,
  post_id uuid NOT NULL,

  user_id uuid NOT NULL
    REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,

  caption text,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

  CONSTRAINT group_post_shares_post_fk
    FOREIGN KEY (post_id, group_id)
    REFERENCES public.group_posts(id, group_id)
    ON DELETE CASCADE,

  CONSTRAINT group_post_shares_unique
    UNIQUE (post_id, user_id),

  CONSTRAINT group_post_shares_caption_check
    CHECK (
      caption IS NULL OR char_length(caption) <= 1000
    )
);

CREATE INDEX group_post_shares_created_idx
  ON public.group_post_shares(created_at DESC);

CREATE INDEX group_post_shares_user_created_idx
  ON public.group_post_shares(user_id, created_at DESC);

-- Fail closed. No table-level client access.
-- All future reads must check the CURRENT Group visibility,
-- original post status, and viewer permissions.

ALTER TABLE public.group_post_shares
  ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

REVOKE ALL ON public.group_post_shares
  FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated;


-- Only approved members of currently public Groups may share.
-- The Group row lock prevents a concurrent privacy change.

CREATE FUNCTION public.groups_share_post(
  p_group_id uuid,
  p_post_id uuid,
  p_caption text DEFAULT NULL
)
RETURNS jsonb
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = ''
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_uid uuid := auth.uid();
  v_group public.groups%ROWTYPE;
  v_post public.group_posts%ROWTYPE;
  v_share_id uuid;
BEGIN
  IF v_uid IS NULL THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'Sign in required'
      USING ERRCODE = '42501';
  END IF;

  SELECT * INTO v_group
  FROM public.groups
  WHERE id = p_group_id
  FOR SHARE;

  IF NOT FOUND
     OR v_group.visibility <> 'public'
     OR NOT parapost_groups_private.allowed(
       p_group_id, 'member'
     )
  THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'Public Group membership required'
      USING ERRCODE = '42501';
  END IF;

  SELECT * INTO v_post
  FROM public.group_posts
  WHERE id = p_post_id
    AND group_id = p_group_id
    AND published
    AND NOT is_deleted;

  IF NOT FOUND THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'Group post unavailable'
      USING ERRCODE = '42501';
  END IF;

  IF parapost_groups_private.blocked(
    v_uid, v_post.author_id
  ) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'Sharing unavailable'
      USING ERRCODE = '42501';
  END IF;

  IF p_caption IS NOT NULL
     AND char_length(p_caption) > 1000 THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'Share caption too long';
  END IF;

  INSERT INTO public.group_post_shares(
    group_id, post_id, user_id, caption
  )
  VALUES (
    p_group_id,
    p_post_id,
    v_uid,
    NULLIF(btrim(p_caption), '')
  )
  ON CONFLICT (post_id, user_id) DO NOTHING
  RETURNING id INTO v_share_id;

  IF v_share_id IS NULL THEN
    SELECT id INTO v_share_id
    FROM public.group_post_shares
    WHERE post_id = p_post_id
      AND user_id = v_uid;
  END IF;

  RETURN jsonb_build_object('id', v_share_id);
END;
$$;

REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION
  public.groups_share_post(uuid, uuid, text)
  FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated;

GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION
  public.groups_share_post(uuid, uuid, text)
  TO authenticated;


-- Returns currently eligible public Group shares.
-- NULL sharer ID: Dashboard feed.
-- Specific sharer ID: that member's Profile.
-- Never copy protected Group content into ordinary posts.

CREATE FUNCTION public.groups_shared_feed(
  p_sharer_id uuid DEFAULT NULL,
  p_limit integer DEFAULT 60,
  p_offset integer DEFAULT 0
)
RETURNS TABLE (
  share_id uuid,
  group_id uuid,
  post_id uuid,
  sharer_id uuid,
  caption text,
  shared_at timestamptz,
  group_name text,
  original_author_id uuid,
  original_content text,
  original_created_at timestamptz,
  media jsonb
)
LANGUAGE sql
STABLE
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = ''
AS $$
  SELECT
    s.id,
    s.group_id,
    s.post_id,
    s.user_id,
    s.caption,
    s.created_at,
    g.name,
    p.author_id,
    p.content,
    p.created_at,
    COALESCE(media_items.items, '[]'::jsonb)
  FROM public.group_post_shares s
  JOIN public.groups g
    ON g.id = s.group_id
  JOIN public.group_posts p
    ON p.id = s.post_id
   AND p.group_id = s.group_id
  LEFT JOIN LATERAL (
    SELECT jsonb_agg(
      jsonb_build_object(
        'media_type', m.media_type,
        'storage_path', m.storage_path,
        'display_order', m.display_order
      )
      ORDER BY m.display_order, m.id
    ) AS items
    FROM public.group_post_media m
    WHERE m.post_id = p.id
      AND m.group_id = g.id
  ) media_items ON true
  WHERE auth.uid() IS NOT NULL
    AND g.visibility = 'public'
    AND p.published
    AND NOT p.is_deleted
    AND parapost_groups_private.allowed(
      g.id, 'read'
    )
    AND NOT parapost_groups_private.blocked(
      auth.uid(), s.user_id
    )
    AND NOT parapost_groups_private.blocked(
      auth.uid(), p.author_id
    )
    AND (
      p_sharer_id IS NULL
      OR s.user_id = p_sharer_id
    )
  ORDER BY s.created_at DESC, s.id DESC
  LIMIT LEAST(GREATEST(COALESCE(p_limit,60),1),100)
  OFFSET LEAST(GREATEST(COALESCE(p_offset,0),0),10000);
$$;

REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION
  public.groups_shared_feed(uuid,integer,integer)
  FROM PUBLIC,anon,authenticated;

GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION
  public.groups_shared_feed(uuid,integer,integer)
  TO authenticated;


COMMIT;


-- ====================================================
-- EXISTING MIGRATION: supabase/migrations/20261024050000_group_shared_post_engagement.sql
-- ====================================================

BEGIN;

CREATE TABLE public.group_post_share_likes (
  share_id uuid NOT NULL
    REFERENCES public.group_post_shares(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  user_id uuid NOT NULL
    REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  PRIMARY KEY (share_id, user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE public.group_post_share_comments (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  share_id uuid NOT NULL
    REFERENCES public.group_post_shares(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  author_id uuid NOT NULL
    REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  parent_id uuid,
  content text NOT NULL,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

  CONSTRAINT group_share_comment_content_check
    CHECK (
      char_length(btrim(content)) BETWEEN 1 AND 1200
    ),

  CONSTRAINT group_share_comment_identity
    UNIQUE (id, share_id),

  CONSTRAINT group_share_comment_parent_fk
    FOREIGN KEY (parent_id, share_id)
    REFERENCES public.group_post_share_comments(id, share_id)
    ON DELETE CASCADE
);

CREATE TABLE public.group_post_share_comment_likes (
  comment_id uuid NOT NULL,
  share_id uuid NOT NULL,
  user_id uuid NOT NULL
    REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

  PRIMARY KEY (comment_id, user_id),

  CONSTRAINT group_share_comment_like_parent_fk
    FOREIGN KEY (comment_id, share_id)
    REFERENCES public.group_post_share_comments(id, share_id)
    ON DELETE CASCADE
);

CREATE INDEX group_share_comments_order_idx
  ON public.group_post_share_comments
  (share_id, created_at, id);

CREATE INDEX group_share_comment_likes_lookup_idx
  ON public.group_post_share_comment_likes
  (share_id, comment_id);

ALTER TABLE public.group_post_share_likes
  ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

ALTER TABLE public.group_post_share_comments
  ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

ALTER TABLE public.group_post_share_comment_likes
  ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

REVOKE ALL ON
  public.group_post_share_likes,
  public.group_post_share_comments,
  public.group_post_share_comment_likes
FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated;


-- Per-user shared-post interaction limits.
-- Two reusable rows per user; no growing request log.
CREATE TABLE parapost_groups_private.shared_post_interaction_rate_limits (
  user_id uuid NOT NULL
    REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  action_kind text NOT NULL
    CHECK (action_kind IN ('comment', 'reaction')),
  window_started_at timestamptz NOT NULL,
  action_count integer NOT NULL
    CHECK (action_count BETWEEN 1 AND 60),
  PRIMARY KEY (user_id, action_kind)
);

ALTER TABLE parapost_groups_private.shared_post_interaction_rate_limits
  ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

REVOKE ALL ON TABLE parapost_groups_private.shared_post_interaction_rate_limits
FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.groups_shared_post_stats(
  p_share_id uuid
)
RETURNS jsonb
LANGUAGE plpgsql
STABLE
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = ''
AS $function$
DECLARE
  v_uid uuid := auth.uid();
  v_group_id uuid;
  v_sharer_id uuid;
  v_author_id uuid;
  v_comments jsonb;
BEGIN
  IF v_uid IS NULL THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'Sign in required'
      USING ERRCODE = '42501';
  END IF;

  SELECT s.group_id, s.user_id, p.author_id
  INTO v_group_id, v_sharer_id, v_author_id
  FROM public.group_post_shares s
  JOIN public.groups g ON g.id = s.group_id
  JOIN public.group_posts p
    ON p.id = s.post_id AND p.group_id = s.group_id
  WHERE s.id = p_share_id
    AND g.visibility = 'public'
    AND NOT p.is_deleted;

  IF NOT FOUND THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'Share unavailable'
      USING ERRCODE = '42501';
  END IF;

  IF NOT parapost_groups_private.allowed(v_group_id, 'read')
     OR parapost_groups_private.blocked(v_uid, v_sharer_id)
     OR parapost_groups_private.blocked(v_uid, v_author_id)
  THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'Share unavailable'
      USING ERRCODE = '42501';
  END IF;

  SELECT COALESCE(
    jsonb_agg(
      jsonb_build_object(
        'id', c.id,
        'author_id', c.author_id,
        'parent_id', c.parent_id,
        'content', c.content,
        'created_at', c.created_at,
        'updated_at', c.updated_at,
        'like_count', c.like_count,
        'liked', c.liked
      )
      ORDER BY c.created_at, c.id
    ),
    '[]'::jsonb
  )
  INTO v_comments
  FROM (
    SELECT
      cm.*,
      (
        SELECT count(*)::integer
        FROM public.group_post_share_comment_likes cl
        WHERE cl.comment_id = cm.id
          AND NOT parapost_groups_private.blocked(v_uid, cl.user_id)
      ) AS like_count,
      EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM public.group_post_share_comment_likes cl
        WHERE cl.comment_id = cm.id
          AND cl.user_id = v_uid
      ) AS liked
    FROM public.group_post_share_comments cm
    WHERE cm.share_id = p_share_id
      AND NOT parapost_groups_private.blocked(v_uid, cm.author_id)
      AND (
        cm.parent_id IS NULL
        OR EXISTS (
          SELECT 1
          FROM public.group_post_share_comments parent
          WHERE parent.id = cm.parent_id
            AND parent.share_id = cm.share_id
            AND parent.parent_id IS NULL
            AND NOT parapost_groups_private.blocked(
              v_uid, parent.author_id
            )
        )
      )
    ORDER BY cm.created_at, cm.id
    LIMIT 150
  ) c;

  RETURN jsonb_build_object(
    'liked', EXISTS (
      SELECT 1
      FROM public.group_post_share_likes l
      WHERE l.share_id = p_share_id
        AND l.user_id = v_uid
    ),
    'like_count', (
      SELECT count(*)::integer
      FROM public.group_post_share_likes l
      WHERE l.share_id = p_share_id
        AND NOT parapost_groups_private.blocked(v_uid, l.user_id)
    ),
    'comment_count', (
      SELECT count(*)::integer
      FROM public.group_post_share_comments c
      WHERE c.share_id = p_share_id
        AND NOT parapost_groups_private.blocked(v_uid, c.author_id)
        AND (
          c.parent_id IS NULL
          OR EXISTS (
            SELECT 1
            FROM public.group_post_share_comments parent
            WHERE parent.id = c.parent_id
              AND parent.share_id = c.share_id
              AND parent.parent_id IS NULL
              AND NOT parapost_groups_private.blocked(
                v_uid, parent.author_id
              )
          )
        )
    ),
    'comments_limited', (
      SELECT count(*) > 150
      FROM public.group_post_share_comments c
      WHERE c.share_id = p_share_id
        AND NOT parapost_groups_private.blocked(v_uid, c.author_id)
        AND (
          c.parent_id IS NULL
          OR EXISTS (
            SELECT 1
            FROM public.group_post_share_comments parent
            WHERE parent.id = c.parent_id
              AND parent.share_id = c.share_id
              AND parent.parent_id IS NULL
              AND NOT parapost_groups_private.blocked(
                v_uid, parent.author_id
              )
          )
        )
    ),
    'comments', v_comments
  );
END;
$function$;

REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.groups_shared_post_stats(uuid)
FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated;

GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.groups_shared_post_stats(uuid)
TO authenticated;

-- Shared Group post interaction function

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.groups_shared_post_interact(
  p_share_id uuid,
  p_action text,
  p_content text DEFAULT NULL,
  p_parent_id uuid DEFAULT NULL
)
RETURNS jsonb
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = ''
AS $function$
DECLARE
  v_uid uuid := auth.uid();
  v_share public.group_post_shares%ROWTYPE;
  v_group public.groups%ROWTYPE;
  v_post public.group_posts%ROWTYPE;
  v_parent public.group_post_share_comments%ROWTYPE;
  v_id uuid;
  v_limit_kind text;
  v_limit_max integer;
  v_limit_now timestamptz;
  v_limit_count integer;
BEGIN
  IF v_uid IS NULL THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'Sign in required'
      USING ERRCODE = '42501';
  END IF;

  SELECT * INTO v_share
  FROM public.group_post_shares
  WHERE id = p_share_id;

  IF NOT FOUND THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'Share unavailable'
      USING ERRCODE = '42501';
  END IF;

  SELECT * INTO v_group
  FROM public.groups
  WHERE id = v_share.group_id
  FOR SHARE;

  IF NOT FOUND
     OR v_group.visibility <> 'public'
     OR NOT parapost_groups_private.allowed(
       v_share.group_id, 'read'
     ) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'Group unavailable'
      USING ERRCODE = '42501';
  END IF;

  SELECT * INTO v_post
  FROM public.group_posts
  WHERE id = v_share.post_id
    AND group_id = v_share.group_id
    AND NOT is_deleted;

  IF NOT FOUND THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'Post unavailable'
      USING ERRCODE = '42501';
  END IF;

  IF parapost_groups_private.blocked(v_uid, v_share.user_id)
     OR parapost_groups_private.blocked(
       v_uid, v_post.author_id
     ) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'Interaction unavailable'
      USING ERRCODE = '42501';
  END IF;

  -- Atomic per-user limits across all shared Group posts.
  IF p_action IN ('comment', 'reply') THEN
    v_limit_kind := 'comment';
    v_limit_max := 10;
  ELSIF p_action IN (
    'like', 'unlike', 'like_comment', 'unlike_comment'
  ) THEN
    v_limit_kind := 'reaction';
    v_limit_max := 60;
  END IF;

  IF v_limit_kind IS NOT NULL THEN
    v_limit_now := clock_timestamp();

    INSERT INTO parapost_groups_private.shared_post_interaction_rate_limits AS rl
      (user_id, action_kind, window_started_at, action_count)
    VALUES (v_uid, v_limit_kind, v_limit_now, 1)
    ON CONFLICT (user_id, action_kind)
    DO UPDATE SET
      window_started_at = CASE
        WHEN rl.window_started_at <=
             v_limit_now - interval '60 seconds'
        THEN v_limit_now
        ELSE rl.window_started_at
      END,
      action_count = CASE
        WHEN rl.window_started_at <=
             v_limit_now - interval '60 seconds'
        THEN 1
        ELSE rl.action_count + 1
      END
    WHERE rl.window_started_at <=
          v_limit_now - interval '60 seconds'
       OR rl.action_count < v_limit_max
    RETURNING action_count INTO v_limit_count;

    IF NOT FOUND THEN
      RAISE EXCEPTION 'Rate limit exceeded. Try again shortly.'
        USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
    END IF;
  END IF;

  IF p_action = 'like' THEN
    INSERT INTO public.group_post_share_likes
      (share_id, user_id)
    VALUES (p_share_id, v_uid)
    ON CONFLICT (share_id, user_id) DO NOTHING;

    RETURN jsonb_build_object('liked', true);

  ELSIF p_action = 'unlike' THEN
    DELETE FROM public.group_post_share_likes
    WHERE share_id = p_share_id
      AND user_id = v_uid;

    RETURN jsonb_build_object('liked', false);

  ELSIF p_action = 'comment' THEN
    IF p_parent_id IS NOT NULL
       OR p_content IS NULL
       OR char_length(btrim(p_content)) NOT BETWEEN 1 AND 1200 THEN
      RAISE EXCEPTION 'Invalid comment';
    END IF;

    INSERT INTO public.group_post_share_comments
      (share_id, author_id, content)
    VALUES (p_share_id, v_uid, btrim(p_content))
    RETURNING id INTO v_id;

    RETURN jsonb_build_object('id', v_id);

  ELSIF p_action IN (
    'reply', 'like_comment', 'unlike_comment',
    'edit_comment', 'delete_comment'
  ) THEN
    IF p_parent_id IS NULL THEN
      RAISE EXCEPTION 'Comment required';
    END IF;

    SELECT * INTO v_parent
    FROM public.group_post_share_comments
    WHERE id = p_parent_id
      AND share_id = p_share_id;

    IF NOT FOUND THEN
      RAISE EXCEPTION 'Comment unavailable'
        USING ERRCODE = '42501';
    END IF;

    IF p_action = 'reply' THEN
      IF v_parent.parent_id IS NOT NULL
         OR parapost_groups_private.blocked(
           v_uid, v_parent.author_id
         )
         OR p_content IS NULL
         OR char_length(btrim(p_content)) NOT BETWEEN 1 AND 1200 THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Reply unavailable'
          USING ERRCODE = '42501';
      END IF;

      INSERT INTO public.group_post_share_comments
        (share_id, author_id, parent_id, content)
      VALUES (
        p_share_id, v_uid, p_parent_id, btrim(p_content)
      )
      RETURNING id INTO v_id;

      RETURN jsonb_build_object('id', v_id);

    ELSIF p_action = 'like_comment' THEN
      IF parapost_groups_private.blocked(
        v_uid, v_parent.author_id
      )
      OR (
        v_parent.parent_id IS NOT NULL
        AND NOT EXISTS (
          SELECT 1
          FROM public.group_post_share_comments parent
          WHERE parent.id = v_parent.parent_id
            AND parent.share_id = p_share_id
            AND parent.parent_id IS NULL
            AND NOT parapost_groups_private.blocked(
              v_uid, parent.author_id
            )
        )
      ) THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Comment Like unavailable'
          USING ERRCODE = '42501';
      END IF;

      INSERT INTO public.group_post_share_comment_likes
        (comment_id, share_id, user_id)
      VALUES (p_parent_id, p_share_id, v_uid)
      ON CONFLICT (comment_id, user_id) DO NOTHING;

      RETURN jsonb_build_object('liked', true);

    ELSIF p_action = 'unlike_comment' THEN
      DELETE FROM public.group_post_share_comment_likes
      WHERE comment_id = p_parent_id
        AND share_id = p_share_id
        AND user_id = v_uid;

      RETURN jsonb_build_object('liked', false);

    ELSIF p_action = 'edit_comment' THEN
      IF v_parent.author_id <> v_uid
         OR p_content IS NULL
         OR char_length(btrim(p_content)) NOT BETWEEN 1 AND 1200 THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Edit unavailable'
          USING ERRCODE = '42501';
      END IF;

      UPDATE public.group_post_share_comments
      SET content = btrim(p_content),
          updated_at = now()
      WHERE id = p_parent_id
        AND share_id = p_share_id
        AND author_id = v_uid;

      RETURN jsonb_build_object('updated', true);

    ELSIF p_action = 'delete_comment' THEN
      IF v_parent.author_id <> v_uid THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Delete unavailable'
          USING ERRCODE = '42501';
      END IF;

      DELETE FROM public.group_post_share_comments
      WHERE id = p_parent_id
        AND share_id = p_share_id
        AND author_id = v_uid;

      RETURN jsonb_build_object('deleted', true);
    END IF;
  END IF;

  RAISE EXCEPTION 'Unknown interaction action';
END;
$function$;

REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION
  public.groups_shared_post_interact(uuid,text,text,uuid)
FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated;

GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION
  public.groups_shared_post_interact(uuid,text,text,uuid)
TO authenticated;

COMMIT;


-- ====================================================
-- EXISTING MIGRATION: supabase/migrations/20261121015929_creatorverse_live_page_link.sql
-- ====================================================

-- CreatorVerse Live: staging-first additive migration.
-- One existing live_streams row represents one broadcast.
-- NULL means an ordinary personal livestream.
-- Do not apply to production without explicit approval.

ALTER TABLE public.live_streams
ADD COLUMN creatorverse_page_id uuid
REFERENCES public.creatorverse_pages(id)
ON DELETE SET NULL;

CREATE INDEX live_streams_creatorverse_page_idx
ON public.live_streams
  (creatorverse_page_id, updated_at DESC)
WHERE creatorverse_page_id IS NOT NULL;

-- Keep the ownership validator outside exposed API schemas.
CREATE SCHEMA IF NOT EXISTS creatorverse_private;

REVOKE ALL ON SCHEMA creatorverse_private FROM PUBLIC;

CREATE FUNCTION creatorverse_private.validate_live_page_owner()
RETURNS trigger
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = ''
AS $function$
BEGIN
  -- Existing personal broadcasts require no new permission.
  IF NEW.creatorverse_page_id IS NULL THEN
    RETURN NEW;
  END IF;

  -- Only the authenticated broadcaster can associate a page.
  IF auth.uid() IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.user_id THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION
      'Unauthorized CreatorVerse livestream association'
      USING ERRCODE = '42501';
  END IF;

  -- The page must be active and belong to the broadcaster.
  IF NOT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1
    FROM public.creatorverse_pages AS p
    WHERE p.id = NEW.creatorverse_page_id
      AND p.owner_id = NEW.user_id
      AND p.status = 'active'
  ) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION
      'CreatorVerse page is not owned or active'
      USING ERRCODE = '42501';
  END IF;

  RETURN NEW;
END;
$function$;

REVOKE ALL
ON FUNCTION creatorverse_private.validate_live_page_owner()
FROM PUBLIC;

CREATE TRIGGER validate_creatorverse_live_page_owner
BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE OF creatorverse_page_id, user_id
ON public.live_streams
FOR EACH ROW
EXECUTE FUNCTION creatorverse_private.validate_live_page_owner();

COMMENT ON COLUMN public.live_streams.creatorverse_page_id IS
  'Optional active CreatorVerse page associated with this broadcast';

-- TABLE 7: public.creatorverse_pages
-- SOURCE: supabase/release-candidates/20261122_creatorverse_base_tables.sql

CREATE TABLE public.creatorverse_pages (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  owner_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES auth.users(id),
  name text NOT NULL CHECK (char_length(btrim(name)) BETWEEN 3 AND 100),
  category text NOT NULL CHECK (
    category IN (
      'business','creator','investigation_team','podcast',
      'entertainment','organization','community_project','other'
    )
  ),
  short_bio text NOT NULL DEFAULT ''
    CHECK (char_length(short_bio) <= 240),
  description text NOT NULL DEFAULT ''
    CHECK (char_length(description) <= 5000),
  website text CHECK (
    website IS NULL OR (
      char_length(website) <= 2048 AND
      website ~ '^https?://[^[:space:]@/]+([/?#][^[:space:]]*)?$'
    )
  ),
  social_links jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]'::jsonb
    CHECK (
      jsonb_typeof(social_links) = 'array'
      AND jsonb_array_length(social_links) <= 8
    ),
  public_email text CHECK (
    public_email IS NULL OR (
      char_length(public_email) <= 254 AND
      public_email ~ '^[^[:space:]@]+@[^[:space:]@]+\.[^[:space:]@]+$'
    )
  ),
  location text NOT NULL DEFAULT ''
    CHECK (char_length(location) <= 160),
  logo_media_id uuid,
  cover_media_id uuid,
  status text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'draft'
    CHECK (status IN ('draft','active','suspended','deleted')),
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  search_document tsvector GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
    to_tsvector(
      'simple'::regconfig,
      name || ' ' || short_bio || ' ' || description
    )
  ) STORED,
  UNIQUE (id, owner_id)
);

-- TABLE 14: public.creatorverse_media
-- SOURCE: supabase/release-candidates/20261122_creatorverse_base_tables.sql

CREATE TABLE public.creatorverse_media (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  page_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES public.creatorverse_pages(id),
  post_id uuid,
  purpose text NOT NULL
    CHECK (purpose IN ('logo','cover','post')),
  media_type text NOT NULL
    CHECK (media_type IN ('image','video')),
  storage_path text NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  mime_type text NOT NULL CHECK (
    mime_type IN (
      'image/jpeg','image/png','image/webp',
      'video/mp4','video/webm'
    )
  ),
  verification text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending'
    CHECK (verification IN ('pending','verified','rejected')),
  byte_size bigint CHECK (byte_size > 0),
  duration_seconds numeric CHECK (
    duration_seconds > 0 AND duration_seconds <= 120
  ),
  display_order smallint NOT NULL DEFAULT 0
    CHECK (display_order BETWEEN 0 AND 3),
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  UNIQUE (page_id, id),
  FOREIGN KEY (page_id, post_id)
    REFERENCES public.creatorverse_posts(page_id, id),
  CHECK (storage_path LIKE page_id::text || '/%'),
  CHECK (
    char_length(storage_path) <= 240
    AND storage_path NOT LIKE '%..%'
    AND storage_path NOT LIKE '%//%'
  ),
  CHECK (
    (purpose = 'post' AND post_id IS NOT NULL)
    OR (
      purpose IN ('logo','cover')
      AND post_id IS NULL
      AND media_type = 'image'
    )
  ),
  CHECK (
    (media_type = 'image'
      AND mime_type IN ('image/jpeg','image/png','image/webp')
      AND duration_seconds IS NULL
      AND (byte_size IS NULL OR byte_size <= 8388608))
    OR
    (media_type = 'video'
      AND mime_type IN ('video/mp4','video/webm')
      AND (byte_size IS NULL OR byte_size <= 33554432))
  ),
  CHECK (
    verification <> 'verified'
    OR (
      byte_size IS NOT NULL
      AND (media_type = 'image' OR duration_seconds IS NOT NULL)
    )
  )
);

-- Fail-closed access baseline:
ALTER TABLE public.creatorverse_media ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
REVOKE ALL ON TABLE public.creatorverse_media
FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated;

-- ====================================================

-- PARAPOST NETWORK - STEP 466
-- RPC AND FUNCTION DEFINITIONS - REVIEW ONLY
-- NOT AN APPROVED OR VERIFIED MIGRATION
--
-- Source: local staging SQL drafts.
-- Existing migration signatures excluded.
-- Conflicting and flagged definitions excluded.
-- Exact live staging definitions are not verified.
-- EXECUTE permissions, ownership, triggers and
-- RLS dependencies remain pending.
-- DO NOT EXECUTE AGAINST PRODUCTION OR STAGING.

-- FUNCTION: public.creatorverse_delete_post(p_page_id uuid, p_post_id uuid)
-- SOURCE: docs/sql-drafts/creatorverse-post-management-STAGING.sql
-- SHA256: 8c7c9fe8a512c9cb2a60f0a478122e0e9d6cd2b2a4896f70cfc7609efa7d7f1c

CREATE FUNCTION public.creatorverse_delete_post(
  p_page_id uuid,
  p_post_id uuid
)
RETURNS uuid
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = ''
AS $body$
DECLARE
  v_user uuid := auth.uid();
  v_post uuid;
BEGIN
  IF v_user IS NULL
     OR NOT creatorverse_private.write_enabled()
     OR NOT creatorverse_private.owns(p_page_id)
     OR NOT creatorverse_private.read_page(p_page_id)
  THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'Post management unavailable'
      USING ERRCODE = '42501';
  END IF;

  PERFORM 1 FROM public.creatorverse_pages
  WHERE id = p_page_id FOR UPDATE;

  SELECT id INTO v_post
  FROM public.creatorverse_posts
  WHERE id = p_post_id
    AND page_id = p_page_id
    AND author_id = v_user
    AND status = 'published'
  FOR UPDATE;

  IF v_post IS NULL THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'Post unavailable'
      USING ERRCODE = '42501';
  END IF;

  UPDATE public.creatorverse_posts
  SET status = 'deleted',
      updated_at = now()
  WHERE id = v_post
  RETURNING id INTO v_post;

  RETURN v_post;
END;
$body$;

-- FUNCTION: public.creatorverse_directory_member_counts(p_page_ids uuid[])
-- SOURCE: docs/sql-drafts/creatorverse-member-counts-STAGING.sql
-- SHA256: 26f17dd90e50bc86baab9c5ad1e9a069e68341b9163ce65088b2121efca4f7b1

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.creatorverse_directory_member_counts(
  p_page_ids uuid[]
)
RETURNS TABLE (
  page_id uuid,
  member_count bigint
)
LANGUAGE sql
STABLE
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = ''
AS $counts$
  SELECT
    p.id,
    COUNT(f.user_id)::bigint
  FROM public.creatorverse_pages AS p
  LEFT JOIN public.creatorverse_follows AS f
    ON f.page_id = p.id
  WHERE cardinality(p_page_ids) BETWEEN 1 AND 25
    AND p.id = ANY(p_page_ids)
    AND p.status = 'active'
    AND creatorverse_private.read_page(p.id)
  GROUP BY p.id;
$counts$;

-- FUNCTION: public.creatorverse_edit_post(p_page_id uuid, p_post_id uuid, p_content text)
-- SOURCE: docs/sql-drafts/creatorverse-post-management-STAGING.sql
-- SHA256: 823902234854d62b020c60ca121d2b82eb8b7e7c2412f6bbaaa36ed142cf836e

CREATE FUNCTION public.creatorverse_edit_post(
  p_page_id uuid,
  p_post_id uuid,
  p_content text
)
RETURNS uuid
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = ''
AS $body$
DECLARE
  v_user uuid := auth.uid();
  v_post uuid;
  v_content text := btrim(p_content);
BEGIN
  IF v_user IS NULL
     OR NOT creatorverse_private.write_enabled()
     OR NOT creatorverse_private.owns(p_page_id)
     OR NOT creatorverse_private.read_page(p_page_id)
  THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'Post management unavailable'
      USING ERRCODE = '42501';
  END IF;

  PERFORM 1 FROM public.creatorverse_pages
  WHERE id = p_page_id FOR UPDATE;

  SELECT id INTO v_post
  FROM public.creatorverse_posts
  WHERE id = p_post_id
    AND page_id = p_page_id
    AND author_id = v_user
    AND status = 'published'
  FOR UPDATE;

  IF v_post IS NULL THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'Post unavailable'
      USING ERRCODE = '42501';
  END IF;

  IF v_content IS NULL
     OR char_length(v_content) > 5000
     OR translate(v_content, E'\n\r\t', '') ~ '[[:cntrl:]]'
  THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'Invalid post content'
      USING ERRCODE = '23514';
  END IF;

  IF v_content = '' AND NOT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1
    FROM public.creatorverse_media
    WHERE page_id = p_page_id
      AND post_id = p_post_id
      AND purpose = 'post'
      AND verification = 'verified'
  ) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'Post cannot be empty'
      USING ERRCODE = '23514';
  END IF;

  UPDATE public.creatorverse_posts
  SET content = v_content,
      updated_at = now()
  WHERE id = v_post
  RETURNING id INTO v_post;

  RETURN v_post;
END;
$body$;

  (id, name, public, file_size_limit)

  ('creatorverse-media', 'creatorverse-media', false, 33554432)
ON CONFLICT (id) DO NOTHING;
